    def __init__(self):

        self.loan_record = LoanRecord

        self.ACCT_MONEY_MARKET = "MM"
        self.ACCT_CD = "CD"
        self.ACCT_IRA = "IR"
        self.LOAN_CURRENT = "C"
        self.LOAN_DELINQUENT = "D"
        self.LOAN_DEFAULT = "X"
        self.LOAN_PAID_OFF = "P"

    def main_control_C1(self) -> None:
        """COBOL logic"""
        self.initialization_C1()
        self.process_banking_C1()
        self.process_loans_C1()
        self.process_insurance_C1()
        self.process_investments_C1()
        self.generate_reports_C1()
        self.termination_C1()
        import sys
        sys.exit()

    def initialization_C1(self) -> None:
        """1000-INITIALIZATION
           PERFORM 1100-open_files
           PERFORM 1200-initialize_counters
           PERFORM 1300-get_current_date"""
           PERFORM 1400-load_parameters"""
           PERFORM 1500-validate_system"""
           DISPLAY "mega_enterprise SYSTEM INITIALIZED"."""
        self.open_files_C1()
        self.initialize_counters_C1()
        self.get_current_date_C1()
        self.load_parameters_C1()
        self.validate_system_C1()
        print("mega_enterprise SYSTEM INITIALIZED")

        pass

    def initialize_counters_C1(self) -> None:
        """1200-initialize_counters"""
           INITIALIZE ws_counters"""
           INITIALIZE ws_totals"""
           INITIALIZE ws_flags."""
        self.ws_counters = WsCounters()
        self.ws_totals = WsTotals()
        self.ws_flags = WsFlags()

    def get_current_date_C1(self) -> None:
        """1300-get_current_date
           ACCEPT ws_current_date FROM DATE YYYYMMDD
           ACCEPT ws_current_time FROM TIME
           STRING ws_current_date DELIMITED SIZE"""
                  '-' DELIMITED SIZE"""
                  ws_current_time DELIMITED SIZE"""
                  INTO ws_current_timestamp."""
        import datetime
        now = datetime.datetime.now()
        self.ws_current_date_data.ws_current_date = int(now.strftime("%Y%m%d"))
        self.ws_current_date_data.ws_current_time = int(now.strftime("%H%M%S%f")[:8])
        self.ws_current_date_data.ws_current_timestamp = now.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H"

def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """4200-calculate_premiums"""
    DISPLAY "CALCULATING PREMIUMS..."
    SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        READ insurance_master NEXT
            AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                PERFORM 4210-determine_base_premium
                PERFORM 4220-apply_risk_factor
                PERFORM 4230-calculate_final_premium
        
    
    """"""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, INSURANCE_MASTER"""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        # Assuming a method to read the next insurance record
        insurance_record: Optional[INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD] = read_insurance_master_next_C2()  # Replace with actual read logic
        if insurance_record is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_record)
            apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_record)
            calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_record)

def determine_base_premium_C2(INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD: INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD) -> None:"""
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    match True:
        case INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_LIFE:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
        case INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_HEALTH:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
        case INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_AUTO:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
        case INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_HOME:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT = INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
        case INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_UMBRELLA:
            WS_CALC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_UMBRELLA_RATE
        case _:
            pass

def apply_risk_factor_C2(INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD: INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_CLAIMS_COUNT > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2(INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD: INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    INSURANCE_MASTER_RECORD.INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_position_value_C2(INVESTMENTS_MASTER: INVESTMENTS_MASTER) -> None:"""
    """COBOL logic"""
    INVESTMENTS_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE = INVESTMENTS_MASTER.INV_QUANTITY * INVESTMENTS_MASTER.INV_CURRENT_PRICE

def calculate_gain_loss_C2(INVESTMENTS_MASTER: INVESTMENTS_MASTER) -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    INVESTMENTS_MASTER.INV_GAIN_LOSS = INVESTMENTS_MASTER.INV_MARKET_VALUE - (INVESTMENTS_MASTER.INV_QUANTITY * INVESTMENTS_MASTER.INV_PURCHASE_PRICE)

def calculate_dividends_C2() -> None:"""

    SET ws_not_eof TO TRUE
    PERFORM UNTIL ws_eof
        READ investment_master NEXT
            AT END SET ws_eof TO TRUE
            NOT AT END
                IF inv_dividend_rate > 0
                    PERFORM 5410-compute_dividend
                    PERFORM 5420-post_dividend
                
        
    
    """"""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, INVESTMENT_MASTER"""
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")

        investment_record: Optional[INVESTMENTS_MASTER] = read_investment_master_next_C2() # Replace with actual read logic

            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            if investment_record.INV_DIVIDEND_RATE > 0:
                compute_dividend_C2(investment_record)
                post_dividend_C2()

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    match True:
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
        case WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
            WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
        case _:
            WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

class CustomerMaster:
    CUST_ID: str = ""
    CUST_NAME: str = ""
    CUST_LAST_NAME: str = ""
    CUST_STATE: str = ""
    CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: int = 0
    CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    CUST_LAST_ACTIVITY: int = 0

@dataclass
class Account:
    ACCT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ACCT_MIN_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

@dataclass
class Loan:
    LOAN_DELINQUENT: bool = False

CUSTOMER_MASTER: CustomerMaster = CustomerMaster()
ACCT: Account = Account()
LOAN: Loan = Loan()
WS_TOTAL_FEES: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_ANNUAL_FEE_CARD: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_DOMESTIC: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_WIRE_FEE_INTL: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_CALC_RESULT: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_SAVINGS_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_PERSONAL_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TEMP_CODE: str = ""
WS_NOT_APPROVED: bool = False
WS_EOF: bool = False
WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0
WS_ERROR_COUNT: int = 0
WS_CURRENT_DATE: int = 0

def cash_flow_forecast_C3() -> None:
    """8911-cash_flow_forecast."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS
    WS_CALC_RESULT = WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS - WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS

WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """9310-customer_segmentation."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF, CUSTOMER_MASTER
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        #Simplified READ customer_master NEXT logic
        #Assuming a function next_customer_master() exists that returns the next customer or None at the end
        next_customer = next_customer_master()
        if next_customer is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            CUSTOMER_MASTER = next_customer  #Update CUSTOMER_MASTER with the next customer
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()

def next_customer_master() -> Optional[CustomerMaster]:
    """Placeholder function for reading the next customer from the customer_master file."""
    #In a real implementation, this would read from a file or database
    #For this example, we'll return None to simulate reaching the end of the file.'
    return None

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """9311-calculate_clv."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_SAVINGS_RATE, WS_PERSONAL_RATE
    WS_CALC_RESULT = (CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE * WS_SAVINGS_RATE) + \
                     (CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_TOTAL_LOANS * WS_PERSONAL_RATE) + \
                     (CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS * Decimal("0.01"))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """9312-assign_segment."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_TEMP_CODE
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 10000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'PLATINUM'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 5000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'GOLD'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 1000:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'SILVER'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_CODE = 'BRONZE'

def default_prediction_C3() -> None:
    """9343-default_prediction."""
    global LOAN, CUSTOMER_MASTER, WS_CALC_RESULT
    if LOAN.LOAN_DELINQUENT:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 25
    if CUSTOMER_MASTER.CUST_CREDIT_SCORE < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """9632-sweep_accounts."""
    global ACCT, WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    if ACCT.ACCT_BALANCE > ACCT.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = ACCT.ACCT_BALANCE - ACCT.ACCT_MIN_BALANCE
        ACCT.ACCT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
        WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

    while not WS_EOF:
        #Simplified READ customer_master NEXT logic
        #Assuming a function next_customer_master() exists that returns the next customer or None at the end
        next_customer = next_customer_master()
        if next_customer is None:
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            CUSTOMER_MASTER = next_customer  #Update CUSTOMER_MASTER with the next customer
            WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1

class TransactionLog:
    """Represents a transaction log record."""
    tran_amount: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")  # Example field

@dataclass
class LoanRecord:
    """Represents a loan record."""
    loan_current_balance: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    loan_paid_off: bool = False

def calculate_pmi_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10335-calculate_pmi."""
    match True:
        case data.WS_LTV_RATIO > 95:
            data.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
        case data.WS_LTV_RATIO > 90:
            data.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
        case data.WS_LTV_RATIO > 85:
            data.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
        case _:
            data.WS_PMI_AMOUNT = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * Decimal('0.0050') / 12

def evaluate_history_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10340-evaluate_history."""
    if data.WS_LATE_90_DAYS > 0:
        data.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 50
        data.WS_FACTOR_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if data.WS_LATE_60_DAYS > 2:
        data.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 30
        data.WS_FACTOR_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if data.WS_LATE_30_DAYS > 5:
        data.WS_RISK_SCORE -= 20
        data.WS_FACTOR_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'

def calculate_final_risk_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10350-calculate_final_risk."""
    data.WS_RISK_SCORE = data.WS_RISK_SCORE // 4  # Integer division
    match True:
        case data.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 80:
            data.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'LOW RISK'
        case data.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 60:
            data.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'MODERATE'
        case data.WS_RISK_SCORE >= 40:
            data.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'ELEVATED'
        case _:
            data.WS_RISK_CATEGORY = 'HIGH RISK'

def determine_approval_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10400-determine_approval."""
    if data.WS_CREDIT_TIER == 'F':
        data.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
        data.WS_CONDITIONS = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if data.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'HIGH RISK':
        data.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
        data.WS_CONDITIONS = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if data.DTI_RATIO > 50:
        data.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'D'
        data.WS_CONDITIONS = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return  # EXIT PARAGRAPH
    data.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS = 'A'
    calculate_approved_terms_C7(data)

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10450-calculate_approved_terms."""
    data.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
    match data.WS_CREDIT_TIER:
        case 'A':
            data.WS_APPROVED_RATE = data.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('0.00')
        case 'B':
            data.WS_APPROVED_RATE = data.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('0.50')
        case 'C':
            data.WS_APPROVED_RATE = data.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('1.50')
        case 'D':
            data.WS_APPROVED_RATE = data.WS_BASE_RATE + Decimal('3.00')
    if data.WS_RISK_CATEGORY == 'ELEVATED':
        data.WS_APPROVED_RATE += Decimal('0.50')

def generate_loan_terms_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10500-generate_loan_terms."""
    data.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE = data.WS_APPROVED_RATE
    data.WS_MONTHLY_RATE = data.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE / 1200
    data.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR = (1 + data.WS_MONTHLY_RATE) ** data.LOAN_TERM_MONTHS
    data.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT = (data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT * data.WS_MONTHLY_RATE * 0  # TODO
                                 data.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR / (data.WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR - 1))
    data.WS_LOAN_PRINCIPAL_BAL = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT

def create_amortization_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10600-create_amortization."""
    data.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
    data.WS_PAYMENT_DATE = get_current_date()
    data.WS_AMORT_IDX = 1
    while data.WS_AMORT_IDX <= data.LOAN_TERM_MONTHS:
        calculate_payment_split_C7(data)
        data.WS_AMORT_IDX += 1

def calculate_payment_split_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10650-calculate_payment_split."""
    data.AMORT_INTEREST[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = data.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE * data.WS_MONTHLY_RATE
    data.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (data.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT - 0  # TODO
                                                     data.AMORT_INTEREST[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1])
    data.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE -= data.AMORT_PRINCIPAL[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1]
    data.AMORT_BALANCE[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = data.WS_RUNNING_BALANCE
    data.AMORT_PAYMENT_NUM[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = data.WS_AMORT_IDX
    data.AMORT_PAYMENT_AMT[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = data.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
    if data.LOAN_MORTGAGE:
        data.AMORT_ESCROW[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = ((data.WS_PROPERTY_TAX + data.WS_INSURANCE_PREMIUM) / 12)
        data.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (data.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT + 0  # TODO
                                                       data.AMORT_ESCROW[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] + 0  # TODO
                                                       data.WS_PMI_AMOUNT)
    else:
        data.AMORT_TOTAL_PMT[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = data.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
    advance_payment_date_C7(data)

def advance_payment_date_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10660-advance_payment_date."""
    data.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH += 1
    if data.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH > 12:
        data.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH = 1
        data.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR += 1
    data.AMORT_PAYMENT_DATE[data.WS_AMORT_IDX - 1] = (data.WS_PAYMENT_YEAR * 10000 + 0  # TODO
                                                      data.WS_PAYMENT_MONTH * 100 + 1)

def finalize_loan_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10700-finalize_loan."""
    data.WS_LOAN_START_DATE = get_current_date()
    data.WS_LOAN_END_DATE = data.WS_LOAN_START_DATE + (data.LOAN_TERM_MONTHS * 30)
    data.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'A'
    create_loan_record_C7(data)
    disburse_funds_C7(data)
    send_confirmation_C7(data)

def create_loan_record_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10750-create_loan_record."""
    data.WS_LOAN_RECORD = WsLoanRecordDef()  # Simulate INITIALIZE
    data.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_id = data.WS_LOAN_ID
    data.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_type = data.WS_LOAN_TYPE
    data.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_amount = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
    data.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_rate = data.WS_LOAN_INTEREST_RATE
    data.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_payment = data.WS_LOAN_MONTHLY_PMT
    data.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_start = data.WS_LOAN_START_DATE
    data.LOAN_RECORD.loan_rec_status = data.WS_LOAN_STATUS
    write_loan_record(data.LOAN_RECORD)

def disburse_funds_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10760-disburse_funds."""
    data.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT = data.WS_LOAN_AMOUNT
    process_deposit(data.WS_DISBURSEMENT_AMOUNT)
    write_audit_trail()

def send_confirmation_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10770-send_confirmation."""
    data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'loan_confirm'
    data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Your loan has been approved'
    send_notification(data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE, data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT)

def process_decline_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10800-process_decline."""
    data.WS_LOAN_STATUS = 'DECLINED'
    record_decline_C7(data)
    send_decline_notice_C7(data)

def record_decline_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10810-record_decline."""
    data.WS_DECLINE_RECORD = WsDeclineRecordDef()
    data.DECLINE_RECORD.decline_loan_id = data.WS_LOAN_ID
    data.DECLINE_RECORD.decline_status = data.WS_APPROVAL_STATUS
    data.DECLINE_RECORD.decline_reason = data.WS_CONDITIONS
    data.DECLINE_RECORD.decline_date = get_current_date()
    write_decline_record(data.DECLINE_RECORD)

def send_decline_notice_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 10820-send_decline_notice."""
    data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'loan_decline'
    data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'LETTER'
    data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Regarding your loan application'
    send_notification(data.WS_NOTIF_TYPE, data.WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, data.WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT)

def portfolio_management_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 11000-portfolio_management."""
    load_portfolio_C7(data)
    update_market_prices_C7(data)
    calculate_values_C7(data)
    rebalance_check_C7(data)
    generate_statements_C7(data)

def load_portfolio_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 11100-load_portfolio."""
    data.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
    data.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while data.WS_HOLD_IDX <= 100 and data.WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        holding_rec = read_holdings_file()
        if holding_rec:
            data.WS_HOLDING[data.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1] = holding_rec
            data.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1
        else:
            data.WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

    data.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT = data.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1

def update_market_prices_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 11200-update_market_prices."""
    data.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
    while data.WS_HOLD_IDX <= data.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
        data.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL = data.WS_HOLDING[data.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1].hold_symbol
        get_quote_C7(data)
        data.WS_HOLDING[data.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1].hold_current_price = data.WS_QUOTE_PRICE
        data.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

def get_quote_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 11250-get_quote."""
    data.QUOTE_REQUEST.quote_request_symbol = data.WS_QUOTE_SYMBOL
    response = get_quote(data.QUOTE_REQUEST)
    if response.quote_response_status == 'OK':
        data.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = response.quote_last_price
    else:
        data.WS_QUOTE_PRICE = Decimal('0.00')

def calculate_values_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 11300-calculate_values."""
    data.WS_TOTAL_VALUE = Decimal('0.00')
    data.WS_COST_BASIS = Decimal('0.00')
    data.WS_UNREALIZED_GAIN = Decimal('0.00')
    data.WS_HOLD_IDX = 1
    while data.WS_HOLD_IDX <= data.WS_HOLDINGS_COUNT:
        calculate_holding_value_C7(data)
        data.WS_HOLD_IDX += 1

def calculate_holding_value_C7(data: MainData) -> None:
    """COBOL: 11350-calculate_holding_value."""
    hold_idx = data.WS_HOLD_IDX - 1
    data.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_market_value = (data.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_shares * 0  # TODO
                                                    data.WS_HOLDING[hold_idx].hold_current_

def calculate_premiums_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13200"""
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(0)
    if 1 <= data.WS_DRIVER_AGE <= 5:
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 200
    elif 6 <= data.WS_DRIVER_AGE <= 10:
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 100
    else:
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += 50
    
    if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 25:
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
    
    if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 0:
        data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR * 200)
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_ACCIDENT_SURCHARGE
    
    if data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR > 0:
        data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE = Decimal(data.WS_VIOLATIONS_3YR * 100)
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM += data.WS_VIOLATION_SURCHARGE
    
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)
    return data


def calc_home_premium_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13230"""
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal(0.003)
    match data.WS_HOME_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 10:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)
        case age if 11 <= age <= 25:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case age if 26 <= age <= 50:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.2)
        case _:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
    
    if data.WS_FLOOD_ZONE == 'Y':
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
    
    if data.WS_SECURITY_SYSTEM == 'Y':
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)
    
    data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT = data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE / Decimal(1000) * Decimal(50)
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM -= data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE_CREDIT
    
    if data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM < 200:
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(200)
    
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM / Decimal(12)
    return data


def calc_health_premium_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13240"""
    data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM = Decimal(300)
    match data.WS_INSURED_AGE:
        case age if 0 <= age <= 18:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.5)
        case age if 19 <= age <= 30:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case age if 31 <= age <= 40:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.3)
        case age if 41 <= age <= 50:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.6)
        case age if 51 <= age <= 60:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.0)
        case _:
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.8)
    
    match data.WS_PLAN_TYPE:
        case 'BRONZE':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.8)
        case 'SILVER':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.0)
        case 'GOLD':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.3)
        case 'PLATINUM':
            data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.6)
    
    if data.WS_FAMILY_PLAN == 'Y':
        data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM *= Decimal(2.5)
    
    data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM = data.WS_BASE_PREMIUM
    data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM = data.WS_MONTHLY_PREMIUM * Decimal(12)
    return data


def underwriting_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13300"""
    data = evaluate_risk_factors_C8(data)
    data = check_medical_history_C8(data)
    data = verify_information_C8(data)
    data = determine_decision_C8(data)
    return data


def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13310"""
    data.WS_RISK_POINTS = 0
    if data.POLICY_LIFE:
        if data.WS_BMI > 30:
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
        if data.WS_SMOKER_FLAG == 'Y':
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 25
        if data.WS_HAZARDOUS_OCCUPATION == 'Y':
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
    if data.POLICY_AUTO:
        if data.WS_DRIVER_AGE < 21:
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        if data.WS_ACCIDENTS_3YR > 1:
            data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 15
    return data


def check_medical_history_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13320"""
    if data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS > 0:
        data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS = Decimal(data.WS_CHRONIC_CONDITIONS * 5)
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += int(data.WS_CONDITION_POINTS)
    if data.WS_RECENT_HOSPITALIZATION == 'Y':
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
    if data.WS_PRESCRIPTION_COUNT > 5:
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 5
    return data


def verify_information_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13330"""
    data = check_fraud_indicators_C8(data)
    data = validate_documents_C8(data)
    return data


def check_fraud_indicators_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13335"""
    if data.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 3:
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 20
        data.WS_FRAUD_FLAG = 'Y'
    if data.WS_ADDRESS_MISMATCH == 'Y':
        data.WS_RISK_POINTS += 10
    return data


def validate_documents_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13336"""
    if data.WS_DOC_MISSING == 'Y':
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'PENDING'
    else:
        data.WS_UW_STATUS = 'COMPLETE'
    return data


def determine_decision_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13340"""
    match True:
        case bool(data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 50):
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'DECLINE'
        case bool(data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 30):
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'SUBSTANDARD'
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal(1.5)
        case bool(data.WS_RISK_POINTS > 15):
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'STANDARD'
        case _:
            data.WS_UW_DECISION = 'PREFERRED'
            data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM *= Decimal(0.9)
    return data


def issue_policy_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13400"""
    if data.WS_UW_DECISION != 'DECLINE':
        data = generate_policy_number_C8(data)
        data = create_policy_record_C8(data)
        data = set_beneficiaries_C8(data)
        data = send_policy_docs_C8(data)
    else:
        data = send_decline_letter_C8(data)
    return data


def generate_policy_number_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13410"""
    data.WS_DATE_PART = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    data.WS_TYPE_PART = data.WS_POLICY_TYPE
    data.WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random.random() * 99999)
    data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER = f"{data.WS_TYPE_PART}{data.WS_DATE_PART}{data.WS_RANDOM_PART}"
    return data


def create_policy_record_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13420"""
    # Assuming WS_POLICY_RECORD is a file write operation, we'll simulate it.'
    data.POLICY_REC_NUMBER = data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
    data.POLICY_REC_TYPE = data.WS_POLICY_TYPE
    data.POLICY_REC_COVERAGE = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
    data.POLICY_REC_PREMIUM = data.WS_ANNUAL_PREMIUM
    data.POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE = data.WS_EFFECTIVE_DATE
    data.POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE = data.WS_EXPIRATION_DATE
    data.POLICY_REC_STATUS = 'A'
    # Simulate writing to file
    print(f"Writing policy record: {data.POLICY_REC_NUMBER}, {data.POLICY_REC_TYPE}, {data.POLICY_REC_COVERAGE}, {data.POLICY_REC_PREMIUM}, {data.POLICY_REC_EFF_DATE}, {data.POLICY_REC_EXP_DATE}, {data.POLICY_REC_STATUS}")
    return data


def set_beneficiaries_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13430"""
    for data.WS_BENEF_IDX in range(1, 6):
        if data.BENEF_NAME[data.WS_BENEF_IDX - 1].strip():
            data.BENEF_REC_POLICY = data.WS_POLICY_NUMBER
            data.BENEF_REC_NAME = data.BENEF_NAME[data.WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            data.BENEF_REC_RELATION = data.BENEF_RELATION[data.WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            data.BENEF_REC_PCT = data.BENEF_PCT[data.WS_BENEF_IDX - 1]
            # Simulate writing to file
            print(f"Writing beneficiary record: {data.BENEF_REC_POLICY}, {data.BENEF_REC_NAME}, {data.BENEF_REC_RELATION}, {data.BENEF_REC_PCT}")
    return data

def claims_handling_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:

def receive_claim_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13510"""
    data.WS_CLAIM_DATE = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    data = generate_claim_number_C8(data)
    data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'RECEIVED'
    return data


def generate_claim_number_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13515"""
    data.WS_DATE_PART = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    data.WS_RANDOM_PART = int(random.random() * 99999)
    data.WS_CLAIM_NUMBER = f"CLM{data.WS_DATE_PART}{data.WS_RANDOM_PART}"
    return data


def validate_claim_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13520"""
    data = check_policy_status_C8(data)
    data = check_coverage_C8(data)
    data = check_deductible_C8(data)
    return data


def check_policy_status_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13522"""
    if data.WS_POLICY_STATUS != 'A':
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'
    return data


def check_coverage_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13524"""
    if data.WS_CLAIM_TYPE != data.WS_COVERED_PERILS:
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'
    return data


def check_deductible_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13526"""
    if data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT <= data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE:
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'DENIED'
        data.WS_CLAIM_DENY_REASON = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'
    return data


def investigate_claim_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13530"""
    if data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT > 10000:
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'INVESTIGATION'
        data = assign_adjuster_C8(data)
    data = fraud_check_C8(data)
    return data


def assign_adjuster_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13535"""
    data.WS_ADJUSTER_ID = 'ADJ001'
    data.WS_NOTES = 'Assigned for investigation'
    return data


def fraud_check_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13536"""
    if data.WS_RECENT_CLAIMS > 2:
        data.WS_FRAUD_REVIEW = 'Y'
    if data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT > data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT * Decimal(0.8):
        data.WS_FRAUD_REVIEW = 'Y'
    return data


def adjudicate_claim_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13540"""
    if data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS != 'DENIED':
        data.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = data.WS_CLAIM_AMOUNT - data.WS_DEDUCTIBLE
        if data.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT > data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT:
            data.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT = data.WS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT
        data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'APPROVED'
    return data


def process_payment_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13550"""
    if data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS == 'APPROVED':
        data = issue_payment_C8(data)
        data = update_claim_record_C8(data)
    return data


def issue_payment_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13555"""
    # Simulate initializing WS_PAYMENT_RECORD
    data.PAY_REC_CLAIM = data.WS_CLAIM_NUMBER
    data.PAY_REC_AMOUNT = data.WS_APPROVED_AMOUNT
    data.PAY_REC_DATE = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    data.PAY_REC_METHOD = 'CHECK'
    # Simulate writing to file
    print(f"Writing payment record: {data.PAY_REC_CLAIM}, {data.PAY_REC_AMOUNT}, {data.PAY_REC_DATE}, {data.PAY_REC_METHOD}")
    return data


def update_claim_record_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 13560"""
    data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS = 'PAID'
    data.WS_CLAIM_CLOSE_DATE = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    # Simulate rewriting the claim record.  In a real system, you'd'
    # likely have an ID to find the record to update
    print(f"Updating claim record: Status={data.WS_CLAIM_STATUS}, Close Date={data.WS_CLAIM_CLOSE_DATE}")
    return data


def payroll_processing_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14000"""
    data = load_employee_data_C8(data)

    return data


def load_employee_data_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14100"""
    data.EMP_SEARCH_KEY = data.WS_EMPLOYEE_ID
    # Simulate reading employee data
    if data.WS_EMPLOYEE_ID == "NOTFOUND":
        data.WS_ERROR_MSG = 'EMPLOYEE NOT FOUND'
        data = handle_error_C8(data)
    else:
        data.WS_EMPLOYEE_REC = "Employee data loaded"
    return data


def calculate_gross_pay_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14200"""
    match data.WS_PAY_TYPE:
        case 'SALARY':
            data = calc_salary_pay_C8(data)
        case 'HOURLY':
            data = calc_hourly_pay_C8(data)
        case 'COMMISSION':
            data = calc_commission_pay_C8(data)
    return data


def calc_salary_pay_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14210"""
    data.WS_GROSS_PAY = data.WS_ANNUAL_SALARY / Decimal(data.WS_PAY_PERIODS)
    return data


def calc_hourly_pay_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14220"""
    if data.WS_HOURS_WORKED <= 40:
        data.WS_REGULAR_PAY = data.WS_HOURS_WORKED * data.WS_HOURLY_RATE
        data.WS_OVERTIME_PAY = Decimal(0)
    else:
        data.WS_REGULAR_PAY = Decimal(40) * data.WS_HOURLY_RATE
        data.WS_OT_HOURS = data.WS_HOURS_WORKED - Decimal(40)
        data.WS_OVERTIME_PAY = data.WS_OT_HOURS * data.WS_HOURLY_RATE * Decimal(1.5)
    data.WS_GROSS_PAY = data.WS_REGULAR_PAY + data.WS_OVERTIME_PAY
    return data


def calc_commission_pay_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14230"""
    data.WS_BASE_PAY = data.WS_BASE_SALARY / Decimal(data.WS_PAY_PERIODS)
    data.WS_COMMISSION_PAY = data.WS_SALES_AMOUNT * data.WS_COMMISSION_RATE
    data.WS_GROSS_PAY = data.WS_BASE_PAY + data.WS_COMMISSION_PAY
    return data


def calculate_taxes_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14300"""
    data = calc_federal_tax_C8(data)
    data = calc_state_tax_C8(data)
    data = calc_local_tax_C8(data)
    data = calc_fica_C8(data)
    return data


def calc_federal_tax_C8(data: DataStore) -> DataStore:
    """Lines: 14310"""
    data.WS_ANNUALIZED_GROSS = data.WS_GROSS_PAY * Decimal(data.WS_PAY_PERIODS)
    data.WS_ALLOWANCE_AMOUNT = Decimal(data.WS_EXEMPTIONS * 4300)
    data.WS_TAXABLE_INCOME = data.WS_ANNUALIZED_GROSS - data.WS_ALLOWANCE_AMOUNT
    if data.WS_TAXABLE_INCOME < 0:
        data.WS_TAXABLE_INCOME = Decimal(0)
    data = apply_tax_brackets_C8(data)
    data.WS_FEDERAL_TAX = data.WS_ANNUAL_TAX / Decimal(data.WS_PAY_PERIODS)
    return data

def suspicious_activity_report_C9(ws_sar_required: str, ws_customer_name: str, ws_customer_address: str, ws_customer_ssn: str, ws_transaction_amount: Decimal) -> None:"""

    """Placeholder for PIN verification"""

def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: str, run_frequency: str) -> int:
    """COBOL logic"""
    match run_frequency:
        case 'DAILY':
            return integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 1
        case 'WEEKLY':
            return integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 7
        case 'MONTHLY':
            return integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 30
        case 'QUARTERLY':
            return integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 90
        case 'YEARLY':
            return integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 365
        case _:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid run frequency: {run_frequency}")

def integer_of_date_C10(date_str: str) -> int:
    """Convert YYYYMMDD to integer date."""
    year = int(date_str[:4])
    month = int(date_str[4:6])
    day = int(date_str[6:8])
    return datetime.date(year, month, day).toordinal()

def data_analytics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    collect_metrics_C10()
    aggregate_data_C10()
    calculate_kpi_C10()
    generate_dashboard_C10()
    export_data_C10()

def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_total_trans_amount, ws_total_trans_count, ws_avg_trans_amount, ws_eof_flag

    ws_total_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_total_trans_count = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    index = 0
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        if index >= len(transaction_file_data):
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_total_trans_count += 1
            ws_total_trans_amount += transaction_file_data[index].trans_amount
            index += 1

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count
    
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_active_customers, ws_new_customers, ws_churned_customers, ws_eof_flag
    ws_active_customers = 0
    ws_new_customers = 0
    ws_churned_customers = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    index = 0
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        if index >= len(customer_file_data):
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if customer_file_data[index].cust_status == 'A':
                ws_active_customers += 1
            if customer_file_data[index].cust_open_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_new_customers += 1
            if customer_file_data[index].cust_close_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_churned_customers += 1
            index += 1

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_response_time_total, ws_response_count, ws_avg_response_time, ws_eof_flag
    ws_response_time_total = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_response_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    index = 0
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        if index >= len(perf_log_file_data):
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_response_time_total += perf_log_file_data[index].perf_response_time
            ws_response_count += 1
            index += 1
    
    if ws_response_count > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def aggregate_data_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    daily_aggregation_C10()
    weekly_aggregation_C10()
    monthly_aggregation_C10()

def daily_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_daily_summary, ws_process_date, ws_total_trans_count, ws_total_trans_amount

    ws_daily_summary = WsDailySummaryC10()
    ws_daily_summary.daily_date = ws_process_date
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount
    ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = Decimal("0.00") #Hardcoded to zeroes as WS variables were not defined
    ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = Decimal("0.00") #Hardcoded to zeroes as WS variables were not defined
    
    #In COBOL write writes to a file but here we just append it to a list
    daily_summary_record = DailySummaryRecordC10(
        daily_date = ws_daily_summary.daily_date,
        daily_trans_count = ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count,
        daily_trans_amount = ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount,
        daily_deposits = ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits,
        daily_withdrawals = ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals
    )
    daily_summary_file_data.append(daily_summary_record)

def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """21220-weekly_aggregation
       IF ws_day_of_week = 7
          INITIALIZE ws_weekly_summary
          MOVE ws_week_number TO weekly_week
          PERFORM 21225-sum_week_data
          WRITE weekly_summary_record FROM ws_weekly_summary
       
    global ws_day_of_week, ws_weekly_summary, ws_week_number
    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary = WsWeeklySummaryC10()
        ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = ws_week_number
        sum_week_data_C10()"""
        #In COBOL write writes to a file but here we just append it to a list"""
        weekly_summary_record = {"""
            "weekly_week": ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week,
            "weekly_trans_count": ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count,
            "weekly_trans_amount": ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount
        }
        print(f"Writing weekly summary: {weekly_summary_record}")

def sum_week_data_C10() -> None:
    """21225-sum_week_data
       MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_count
       MOVE ZEROES TO weekly_trans_amount
       PERFORM 7 TIMES
          ADD daily_trans_count TO weekly_trans_count
          ADD daily_trans_amount TO weekly_trans_amount
       """
    global ws_weekly_summary"""
    ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0"""
    ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    for _ in range(7):
        if daily_summary_file_data:
          ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count
          ws_weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """21230-monthly_aggregation
       IF ws_end_of_month = 'Y'
          INITIALIZE ws_monthly_summary
          MOVE ws_curr_month TO monthly_month
          MOVE ws_curr_year TO monthly_year
          PERFORM 21235-sum_month_data
          WRITE monthly_summary_record FROM ws_monthly_summary
       
    global ws_end_of_month, ws_monthly_summary, ws_curr_month, ws_curr_year
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary = WsMonthlySummaryC10()
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = ws_curr_month
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = ws_curr_year
        sum_month_data_C10()"""
        #In COBOL write writes to a file but here we just append it to a list"""
        monthly_summary_record = {"""
            "monthly_month": ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month,
            "monthly_year": ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year,
            "monthly_trans_count": ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count,
            "monthly_trans_amount": ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount,
            "monthly_new_accounts": ws_monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts,
            "monthly_closed_accounts": ws_monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts
        }
        print(f"Writing monthly summary: {monthly_summary_record}")

def sum_month_data_C10() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_monthly_summary, ws_eof_flag, ws_curr_month, ws_daily_sum_rec
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0
    ws_monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    index = 0
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        if index >= len(daily_summary_file_data):
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file_data[index]
            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_month == ws_curr_month:
                ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                ws_monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount
            index += 1

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_SHIP_METHOD, WS_SHIP_EST_DELIVERY
    if True:  # Replace with your condition
        WS_SHIP_METHOD = 'EXPRESS'
        WS_SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = WS_PROCESS_DATE + 2  # Assuming integer_of_date is just the integer itself
    else:
        WS_SHIP_METHOD = 'STANDARD'
        WS_SHIP_EST_DELIVERY = WS_PROCESS_DATE + 7
    # WRITE shipment_record FROM ws_shipment_record. (Implementation depends on file handling)

    """COBOL logic"""

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    validate_wire_request_C11() # PERFORM 24100-validate_wire_request
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11() # PERFORM 24200-ofac_screening
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11() # PERFORM 24300-process_wire
            send_confirmation_C11() # PERFORM 24400-send_confirmation
        else:
            reject_wire_C11() # PERFORM 24500-reject_wire

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == "":
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_WIRE_REJECT
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST(), OFAC_RESPONSE())  # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST(), OFAC_RESPONSE())  # CALL 'OFACSRCH' USING ofac_request ofac_response
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    debit_originator_C11() # PERFORM 24310-debit_originator
    create_wire_message_C11() # PERFORM 24320-create_wire_message
    transmit_wire_C11() # PERFORM 24330-transmit_wire
    record_wire_C11() # PERFORM 24340-record_wire

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11() # PERFORM 2350-update_account

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS
    swift_send_C11(WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE)  # CALL 'SWIFTSEND' USING ws_swift_message ws_swift_response
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11() # PERFORM 24350-reverse_debit

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_DATE, WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES

    # READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_file_header (Implementation depends on file handling)
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_FILE_ID
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_CREATION_DATE
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ENTRY_COUNT

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        # READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
        try:
            # Assuming read_ach_entry() reads from ach_input_file and returns the data
            WS_ACH_ENTRY = read_ach_entry_C11() # type: ignore
            validate_single_entry_C11() # PERFORM 25210-validate_single_entry
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isdigit():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == "":
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        # READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
        try:
            # Assuming read_ach_entry() reads from ach_input_file and returns the data
            WS_ACH_ENTRY = read_ach_entry_C11() # type: ignore
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('22', '23', '32', '33'):
                apply_credit_C11() # PERFORM 25310-apply_credit
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_credit_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11() # PERFORM 5000-search_account
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """29100-validate_stop_request."""COBOL logic"""29200-create_stop_order."""
    global WS_STOP_RECORD, ACCT_ID, WS_CHECK_NUMBER, WS_CHECK_AMOUNT, WS_PAYEE_NAME, WS_PROCESS_DATE, STOP_EXPIRY_DATE, STOP_STATUS, STOP_RECORD
    WS_STOP_RECORD = WsStopRecord()  # INITIALIZE ws_stop_record
    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_account = ACCT_ID  # MOVE acct_id TO stop_account
    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_check_number = WS_CHECK_NUMBER  # MOVE ws_check_number TO stop_check_number
    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_amount = WS_CHECK_AMOUNT  # MOVE ws_check_amount TO stop_amount
    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_payee = WS_PAYEE_NAME  # MOVE ws_payee_name TO stop_payee
    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_effective_date = WS_PROCESS_DATE  # MOVE ws_process_date TO stop_effective_date
    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_expiry_date = int(datetime.datetime.strptime(str(WS_PROCESS_DATE), '%Y%m%d').toordinal()) + 180 # FUNCTION integer_of_date(ws_process_date) + 180
    WS_STOP_RECORD.stop_status = 'A'  # MOVE 'A' TO stop_status
    STOP_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_STOP_RECORD
    #WRITE stop_record FROM ws_stop_record
    print(f"Writing stop record: {STOP_RECORD}")

def apply_stop_fee_C12() -> None:
    """29300-apply_stop_fee."""
    global WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_CHECK_NUMBER
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_STOP_PAYMENT_FEE  # SUBTRACT ws_stop_payment_fee FROM ws_account_balance
    update_account_2350_C12()  # PERFORM 2350-update_account
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'stop_payment'  # MOVE 'stop_payment' TO ws_notif_type
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'  # MOVE 'EMAIL' TO ws_notif_channel
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = f"Stop payment placed on check # {WS_CHECK_NUMBER}"  # STRING 'Stop payment placed on check #' DELIMITED SIZE ...
    send_notification_15000_C12()  # PERFORM 15000-send_notification

def safe_deposit_box_30000_C12() -> None:
    """30000-safe_deposit_box."""COBOL logic"""30100-box_rental."""COBOL logic"""30110-check_availability."""COBOL logic"""30120-assign_box."""COBOL logic"""30130-create_rental_agreement."""
    global WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT, WS_ASSIGNED_BOX, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, WS_PROCESS_DATE, RENTAL_ANNUAL_FEE, WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE, WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, RENTAL_RECORD
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT = WsRentalAgreement()  # INITIALIZE ws_rental_agreement
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_box_number = WS_ASSIGNED_BOX  # MOVE ws_assigned_box TO rental_box_number
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_customer = WS_CUSTOMER_ID  # MOVE ws_customer_id TO rental_customer
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_start_date = WS_PROCESS_DATE  # MOVE ws_process_date TO rental_start_date
    WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT.rental_annual_fee = WS_BOX_SIZE_FEE.get(WS_REQUESTED_SIZE, Decimal("0.00"))  # COMPUTE rental_annual_fee = ws_box_size_fee(ws_requested_size)
    RENTAL_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_RENTAL_AGREEMENT
    #WRITE rental_record FROM ws_rental_agreement
    print(f"Writing rental record: {RENTAL_RECORD}")

def notify_renter_30330_C12() -> None:
    """30330-notify_renter."""COBOL logic"""30400-box_billing."""COBOL logic"""30410-charge_annual_fee."""
    global WS_BOX_IDX, BOX_RENTER, WS_CUSTOMER_ID, BOX_ANNUAL_FEE, WS_FEE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL
    WS_CUSTOMER_ID = BOX_RENTER.get(WS_BOX_IDX)  # MOVE box_renter(ws_box_idx) TO ws_customer_id
    WS_FEE_AMOUNT = BOX_ANNUAL_FEE.get(WS_BOX_IDX, Decimal("0.00"))  # MOVE box_annual_fee(ws_box_idx) TO ws_fee_amount
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= WS_FEE_AMOUNT  # SUBTRACT ws_fee_amount FROM ws_account_balance
    update_account_2350_C12()  # PERFORM 2350-update_account
    BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL[WS_BOX_IDX] = BOX_NEXT_RENEWAL.get(WS_BOX_IDX,0) + 10000  # COMPUTE box_next_renewal(ws_box_idx) = box_next_renewal(ws_box_idx) + 10000

#33200-update_cfp_status
#             MOVE 'ADEQUATE' TO ws_cfp_status
def update_cfp_status_C14(ws_cfp_status: str) -> str:
    """33200-update_cfp_status."""COBOL logic"""33330-update_cfp_document."""COBOL logic"""34000-capital_management."""COBOL logic"""34100-calculate_capital_ratios."""COBOL logic"""34110-calculate_tier1."""COBOL logic"""34120-calculate_tier2."""COBOL logic"""34130-calculate_ratios."""COBOL logic"""34200-risk_weighted_assets."""COBOL logic"""34210-credit_rwa."""COBOL logic"""34220-market_rwa."""COBOL logic"""34230-operational_rwa."""COBOL logic"""34300-capital_planning."""COBOL logic"""34310-project_capital_needs."""COBOL logic"""34320-identify_capital_actions."""
    ws_capital_action: str = ""
    if ws_capital_gap > Decimal('0'):
        if ws_capital_gap <= ws_retained_earnings_proj:
            ws_capital_action = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif ws_capital_gap <= ws_sub_debt_capacity:
            ws_capital_action = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            ws_capital_action = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        ws_capital_action = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'
    return ws_capital_action

#34330-update_capital_plan
#           MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ws_plan_update_date
#           MOVE ws_capital_action TO plan_recommended_action
#           MOVE ws_capital_gap TO plan_gap_amount
#           REWRITE capital_plan_record FROM ws_capital_plan.
def update_capital_plan_C14(ws_capital_action: str, ws_capital_gap: Decimal, capital_plan_record: dict) -> dict:
    """34330-update_capital_plan."""COBOL logic"""34400-stress_testing."""COBOL logic"""34410-run_baseline."""COBOL logic"""34420-run_adverse."""COBOL logic"""34430-run_severely_adverse."""COBOL logic"""34440-compile_results."""COBOL logic"""34450-calculate_stress_impact."""
    ws_credit_losses: Decimal = ws_loan_portfolio * ws_stress_lgd * ws_stress_pd
    ws_market_losses: Decimal = ws_trading_assets * ws_rate_shock / Decimal('100')
    ws_stress_losses: Decimal = ws_credit_losses + ws_market_losses
    ws_stressed_capital: Decimal = ws_total_capital - ws_stress_losses
    ws_stressed_ratio: Decimal = (ws_stressed_capital / ws_risk_weighted_assets) * Decimal('100')

    ws_stress_pass_fail: str = ""
    if ws_stressed_ratio >= ws_min_capital_ratio:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'PASS'
    else:
        ws_stress_pass_fail = 'FAIL'

    return ws_stress_pass_fail

#34460-remediation_actions
#           MOVE 'stress_failure' TO ws_notif_type
#           MOVE 'EMAIL' TO ws_notif_channel
#           MOVE 'URGENT: Stress test failure - action required'
#              TO ws_notif_subject
#           PERFORM 15000-send_notification.
def remediation_actions_C14():
    """34460-remediation_actions."""COBOL logic"""35000-general_ledger."""COBOL logic"""35100-post_journal_entry."""COBOL logic"""35110-validate_journal_entry."""
    ws_je_valid: str = 'Y'
    ws_total_debits: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_total_credits: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_je_error: str = ""

    for ws_je_idx in range(len(je_debit)):
        ws_total_debits += je_debit[ws_je_idx]
        ws_total_credits += je_credit[ws_je_idx]

    if ws_total_debits != ws_total_credits:
        ws_je_valid = 'N'
        ws_je_error = 'OUT OF BALANCE'

    return ws_je_valid

#35120-post_to_accounts
#           PERFORM VARYING ws_je_idx FROM 1 BY 1
#              UNTIL ws_je_idx > 50
#              IF je_gl_account(ws_je_idx) NOT  = None  # TODO: was SPACES
#                 MOVE je_gl_account(ws_je_idx) TO ws_gl_account
#                 READ gl_master_file INTO ws_gl_record
#                    KEY IS gl_account
#                 ADD je_debit(ws_je_idx) TO ws_gl_debit_balance
#                 ADD je_credit(ws_je_idx) TO ws_gl_credit_balance
#                 COMPUTE ws_gl_net_balance = 
#                    ws_gl_debit_balance - ws_gl_credit_balance
#                 REWRITE gl_record FROM ws_gl_record
#              
#           
def post_to_accounts_C14(je_gl_account: list[str], je_debit: list[Decimal], je_credit: list[Decimal], gl_master_file: dict) -> dict:
    """35120-post_to_accounts."""COBOL logic"""35130-record_posting."""COBOL logic"""35200-balance_gl."""
    ws_total_assets: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_total_liabilities: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_total_equity: Decimal = Decimal('0')
    ws_eof_flag: str = 'N'
    ws_error_msg: str = ""
    
    for ws_gl_account, ws_gl_record in gl_master_file.items():
      ws_gl_net_balance: Decimal = ws_gl_record.get('ws_gl_net_balance', Decimal('0'))

      if ws_gl_account.startswith(gl_asset):
        ws_total_assets += ws_gl_net_balance
      elif ws_gl_account.startswith(gl_liability):
        ws_total_liabilities += ws_gl_net_balance
      elif ws_gl_account.startswith(gl_equity):
        ws_total_equity += ws_gl_net_balance
    
    ws_balance_check: Decimal = ws_total_assets - ws_total_liabilities - ws_total_equity
    if ws_balance_check != Decimal('0'):
        ws_error_msg = 'GL OUT OF BALANCE'
        handle_error_C14(ws_error_msg)

def handle_error_C14(ws_error_msg: str):
  print(f"ERROR: {ws_error_msg}")

    """38410-move_to_archive"""

            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
        else:
            if audit_record['timestamp'] < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE:  # Assuming audit_record is a dictionary with 'timestamp' key
                write_archive_audit_record(audit_record)
                delete_audit_file()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'"""
